Migrate BreadcrumbManagerTabHelper off WebContentsUserData

Convert BreadcrumbManagerTabHelper from WebContentsUserData to
UnownedUserData owned by Desktop and Android TabFeatures via
std::unique_ptr, and remove it from the PRESUBMIT.py
_ALLOWED_CREATE_FOR_CALLS allowlist.

Update BreadcrumbManagerBrowserAgent to look up the tab's breadcrumb ID
via tabs::TabInterface* instead of content::WebContents*:
- For TabStripModelChange::kReplaced, pass the discarded helper's unique
  ID into the replacement BreadcrumbManagerTabHelper during
  TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents so kReplaced can log both the old and
  new tab breadcrumb IDs after the discard completes.
- For TabStripModelChange::kRemoved, cache the closing tab's breadcrumb
  ID in OnTabWillBeRemoved before RemoveTabFromIndexImpl tears down
  TabFeatures.

Move some windows compilator builders to to windows 11

This moves the bots and and their associated CI builders to win11.

Moves win-arm64-dbg to windows-default, win-arm64-compile-dbg, and
win_chromium_compile_dbg_ng so they can be overwritten here and on
branch builders when cherryicked.

win-libfuzzer-asan-rel and Win Builder (dbg) don't get overwritten as they are on windows_any now and on branch builders.

We're moving to windows-11 and will follow the following plan:
  -Before landing this CL, move infra groups to win-11, having
    win10 and win11 builder bots available.
  -Cherry pick change to branches.
  -Remove the win10 builder pools after transition.
  -After the builders have migrated, the windows default will be
   moved to windows 11 and clear windows-default.json.

Moves:
try bots:
    win-arm64-compile-dbg
    win-arm64-dbg
    win_chromium_compile_dbg_ng
    win-libfuzzer-asan-rel
CI bots
    Win Builder (dbg)
    Libfuzzer Upload Windows ASan
    win-arm64-dbg

[Vertical Tabs] Hide drag shadow over non-originating window's rail

The external drag shadow should only be visible when a drag will land
outside of an existing Chrome window.

View#updateDragShadow only works from the window that started the drag.
AnimatedDragShadowBuilder preferred the caller's view, which in a
non-originating window silently no-ops. Prefer originating-window views
and use the caller's view only as a last resort.

vr: avoid glFinish() in the OpenXR render loop on Linux
📋 Metadata
commit
acc4a4d1a59f1a4b7f70bb27529094781e2eb699
[ log ]
author
utzcoz <utzcoz@gmail.com>
Mon Oct 12 21:12:17 2026
committer

Mon Oct 12 21:12:17 2026
tree
d234152f3dc8e78daf733599a2f7b261e0447230
parent
c0e96e83d862a8b14d9a6c8122903e871f9d1679 [ diff ] 
vr: avoid glFinish() in the OpenXR render loop on Linux

Without GL_CHROMIUM_gpu_fence, which on Linux means ANGLE on GLX, the
X11 default, the render loop calls glFinish() to wait for the page's
frame. That blocks the GPU main thread and the render loop for as long
as the frame takes to draw.

The shared images on Linux are DMA-BUFs, which carry the fences of
their writers. So flush the GL writes and wait in Vulkan on the write
fence exported from the DMA-BUF. That is the fence signaled by GL and
waited on by Vulkan that the TODO asked for, so drop the TODO.

The render loop only waits on that fence while it is pending. Without
a pending fence the write may be done, or the GL driver may not attach
its fences at all. Then, and on kernels before 6.0, which cannot export
the fence, it calls glFinish() as before.

All of this is under IS_LINUX. The other platforms are unchanged.

tree

parent
5d68ff6c65313038b0840cc37683bc43480bc985 [ diff ] 
media/gpu/v4l2: Allow VPU devices that also advertise JPEG to be enumerated as decoders

Some VPUs may expose JPEG alongside H264 and other formats on the
same OUTPUT queue. EnumerateDevicesForType() previously rejected
such devices for kDecoder/kEncoder because of the !is_jpeg_format()
guard in the all_of predicate.

A device is a valid video decoder as long as none of its input
formats are raw pixel formats and at least one is a video codec
format. Whether JPEG is also present is irrelevant. Drop the
redundant JPEG check from the kDecoder/kEncoder path to fix
hardware video decoder selection on these platforms.

R=frkoenig@chromium.org, nhebert@chromium.org

📋 Metadata

[ log ]
author
Mikel Astiz <mastiz@chromium.org>

Mon Oct 12 21:08:10 2026
tree
595324bc111467130fd9a6c19e756eb1155fb4db
parent
be1831251cdc35619fb6fe77bb976f9cfcab6941 [ diff ] 
[sync] Wait for passkey commits in PRE_*PasskeysPersistsOverRestarts

In PRE_UpdatingPasskeysPersistsOverRestarts and
PRE_DeletingPasskeysPersistsOverRestarts, AddNewPasskeyForTesting()
commits a passkey and triggers a self-invalidation with
notification_hint = "hint". The tests then call UpdatePasskey() or
DeletePasskey() and exit immediately without waiting for the second
mutation to be committed.

If the mutation is not committed before PRE_ shutdown, the persisted
pending invalidation ("hint") from the initial AddNewPasskeyForTesting()
commit causes a GetUpdates cycle on restart to download the older server
entity before the local mutation is committed, which can overwrite the
uncommitted local update.

Wait for the updated or deleted passkey state to be committed to
FakeServer before exiting both PRE_ tests.

tree
3acdfa6dfa848c92bc1712ee42131dc72932c7d5
parent
39ea056a2a18f115574727b35d785fea0a076b24 [ diff ] 
WebTransport: Make WebTransport Datagram writable transferable

Route WebTransportDatagramsWritable through WritableStream's existing
transfer implementation while keeping its IDL consistent with the
WebTransport send and receive stream subclasses.

After a successful transfer, the source is locked and the destination
receives a plain WritableStream. WebTransport does not currently define
subclass-specific transfer-receiving steps, so datagram-specific state
such as sendGroup and sendOrder is not preserved on the destination.

6831a617dd969ea7e258902bb7f615354ca65d0f [ diff ] 
WebUILocationBar: Fix BrowserFocusTest.NavigateFromOmnibox

BrowserFocusTest.NavigateFromOmnibox fails with WebUILocationBar on its
EXPECT_FALSE(view->IsSelectAll()) checks after a navigation is started
from the omnibox (regression test for crbug.com/40672172).

Both omnibox implementations go through the same synchronous sequence
when Enter is pressed: OpenMatch() -> Navigate() ->
UpdateUIForNavigationInTab() runs ToolbarView::Update() while the
omnibox is still focused, so Update() does RevertAll() + SelectAll(true),
and then contents->SetInitialFocus() moves Views focus to the web
contents, which blurs the omnibox in the same task (for the WebUI
location bar via WebUIToolbarInternalWebView::OnBlur() ->
WebUILocationBar::OnBlur() -> WebUIReadOnlyOmnibox::OnBlur()). Nothing
is ever painted selected in either case.

The two differ only in what OnBlur() does with the selection.
OmniboxViewViews stashes it in saved_selection_for_focus_change_ (to
restore on refocus) and collapses the displayed selection, which is what
IsSelectAll() reports. WebUIReadOnlyOmnibox keeps it in selection_, as
does the renderer's <input>, which is neither shown (readonly_omnibox.ts
switches to the static text view) nor painted with a selection
(FrameSelection::IsHidden()) while unfocused. So under WebUILocationBar,
IsSelectAll() reports the counterpart of what OmniboxViewViews stashes,
not of what it displays, and the assertion is checking a Views Textfield
display detail rather than the user-visible property the test guards.
IsSelectAll() has no non-test callers outside OmniboxViewViews itself.

Collapsing selection_ in WebUIReadOnlyOmnibox::OnBlur() (an earlier
patch set) is not a good fix: it changes nothing visible, and because
OnBlur() pushes the state to the WebUI and ui_version_ is 0 after any
RevertAll() or after the Ctrl+L focus-request path, the collapsed
selection reaches the renderer's <input> and discards the selection it
would otherwise restore on window re-activation (which Views restores
from saved_selection_for_focus_change_).

Instead, make the test check the WebUI-equivalent observable: once the
navigation has started, the omnibox is no longer focused, so the <input>
cannot be showing a selection. Keep the IsSelectAll() checks for the
Views omnibox. The check lives in
BrowserFocusTest::ExpectOmniboxTextNotShownSelected() so the two
implementations are handled in one place.

Only post-blur IsSelectAll()==false assertions are affected by this
difference. The other test callers that run with WebUILocationBar
(omnibox_view_interactive_uitest.cc, autocomplete_browsertest.cc) check
the selection while the omnibox is focused or right after a browser-side
RevertAll()/SelectAll(), where WebUIReadOnlyOmnibox::selection_ is
authoritative; the remaining callers use OmniboxViewViews directly.

[WebUI Toolbar] Fix WebUILocationBarInteractiveUiTest
📋 Metadata

tree
455feb5301a5434f7177b1c2313f7e999ac079fe
parent
11f0c566a36dea1cb066fde78b875761a6831a68 [ diff ] 
[WebUI Toolbar] Fix WebUILocationBarInteractiveUiTest

`WebUILocationBarInteractiveUiTest.PasteSanitizesText` was flaky on
macOS for two reasons:

1. It did not wait for `WaitTillOmniboxViewFocus()` before triggering
the paste. In both Cutout (`readonly_omnibox.ts`) and Full
(`omnibox_popup_searchbox.ts`) modes, the input text ("about:blank") and
selection range (0, 11) can be applied to `#input` before DOM focus
actually lands (e.g. when focus is deferred until document visibility
changes or IPC completion). As a result, the paste could be triggered
before `#input` was focused.

2. On macOS, `InteractionTestUtilSimulatorBrowser::SendAccelerator`
ignores the target `TrackedElement` (`InputWebContents()`) and sends the
key event to `BrowserView`'s native window via
`ui_controls::SendKeyPress`. This is prone to dropping `Cmd+V` when the
popup widget window (in Full mode) or the toolbar `WebView`'s
`firstResponder` state (in Cutout mode) has not yet settled.

Fix this by waiting for `WaitTillOmniboxViewFocus()` and invoking
`WebContents::Paste()` directly on `InputWebContents()`, which
dispatches the DOM `paste` `ClipboardEvent` in Blink and exercises the
full WebUI paste sanitization pipeline without relying on OS-level
accelerator routing.

Fixed: 569476564

[Reading mode PDFs] Use character rotation for text run direction

In `PDFiumPage::CalculateTextRunInfoAt()`, inferring text run direction
solely from the angle between character centers can misclassify short
horizontal runs (e.g. "1.") as vertical when adjacent characters have
different heights. When `PdfAccessibilityHeuristicEnhancements` is
enabled, use the character rotation direction unless unrotated
characters advance right-to-left.

17779f8187b95726697e7cf4b9baaf6d327d3dc8 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Fix omnibox focus when extension side panel is shown

When an extension side panel is shown (e.g., an extension using
autofocus or async DOM focus), the omnibox could remain visibly focused
with a blinking cursor while the side panel captured keyboard input.
Clicking on the omnibox subsequently failed to refocus it, leaving
keystrokes trapped in the side panel.

Bug:
https://drive.google.com/file/d/1jvdVAUAcP4IckbY4iuX62MSOytZkMbJD/view

Fixed demo:
https://drive.google.com/file/d/18ZHC0tEPrn7skLBLErxketwXhSCsXfUH/view

This bug only occurred on Linux and Windows . To reproduce, you must
open a new tab, then open the extension side panel, (notice that both
the omnibox and input section in the side panel are both focused), click
inside the omnibox, and notice that typed text would only show up in the
side panel input instead of switching over to the omnibox. This change
removed the autofocus logic added in crrev.com/c/7640468 which bypassed
views::FocusManager leaving the FocusManager’s internal state thinking
that OmniboxViewViews was still the focused view. When the user clicked
back on the omnibox, FocusManager treated the focus request as a no-op,
preventing proper native focus transition and input routing.

This CL resolves the issue by: 1. Removing the direct
host_->host_contents()->Focus() from
ExtensionSidePanelCoordinator::OnEntryShown() 2.
RestrictingSetVisible(false) in
ExtensionViewViews::OnWebContentsAttached() to only apply to
kExtensionPopup keeps the side panel view visible allows the
FocusManager to properly manage focus transitions.

[ContextualTasks] Fix extension reload UI test

`ContextualTasksExtensionsInteractiveUiTest` interacts with
`ExtensionsMenuMainPageView` (`kExtensionsMenuMainPageElementId`) and
its site access toggle (`kExtensionsMenuEntryViewElementId`) to trigger
the side panel reload dialog. While `kExtensionsMenuAccessControl` is
enabled via `fieldtrial_testing_config.json` on non-branded trybots,
branded CI builders like `linux-chromeos-chrome` disable field trial
testing configs and fall back to the legacy `ExtensionsMenuView`,
causing `WaitForShow(kExtensionsMenuMainPageElementId)` to time out.
Explicitly enable `kExtensionsMenuAccessControl` in the test fixture.

97f4ed175f2809327320ac566c9a5229bd4609df [ diff ] 
[Facilitated Payments] Implement OnBarcodesDetected on the factory

When the renderer's heuristic signals qualify a page,
`ContentFacilitatedPaymentsDriverFactory` now extracts the page images
and decodes them with `wallet::WalletablePassBarcodeDetector`, passing
the QR code size and aspect ratio limits from Finch (`min_image_size`,
`max_aspect_ratio`).

When a QR code is decoded, the factory latches the page as detected and
turns off the renderer's heuristic scans.

Remove more unneeded deps from chrome/test/BUILD.gn

Now that many test sources have moved out of chrome/test/BUILD.gn into
per-directory targets, several deps of unit_tests, browser_tests and
interactive_ui_tests are no longer needed by the remaining sources.

buildflags, Java and other data or generated-header deps were kept
even when gn check didn't require them, as were test_support deps

and after for unit_tests, browser_tests and interactive_ui_tests on
Linux and ChromeOS, and for unit_tests on Android desktop.

tree
4574e3c146ebd0ec26954ef89c465bffb15f276a
parent
9d845c63049fa452faff500fb3b6b2902026f239 [ diff ] 
views/a11y: Only perform actions on nodes in the client tree

ViewsAXManager::PerformAction() resolved its target via
AXTreeSourceViews::GetFromId() and dispatched actions even when the
target had been pruned from the client tree, such as when an ancestor
window was marked with kAXConsiderInvisibleAndIgnoreChildren. Because
cached wrappers are retained until object destruction and AXVirtualView
identifiers remain resolvable while their owning view is alive, actions
could be dispatched to nodes not currently present in the client tree.

Align the action path with the event path by verifying that the
resolved target is present in the client tree before dispatching the
action. Target resolution logic is consolidated into
AXTreeSourceViews::GetActionTarget() to be shared across action
handlers.

AX-Relnotes: n/a.

parent
eb9b22739fd66616b741887005c72e10ddbad0ce [ diff ] 
[Organizer] Fix AppearsInTabStripWhenVerticalTabsExpandOnHover on Win

In OrganizerPanelUiTest.AppearsInTabStripWhenVerticalTabsExpandOnHover,
MoveMouseTo(kTabStripRegionElementId) targets the vertical center of
VerticalTabStripRegionView. When the vertical tab strip is collapsed,
that point falls inside VerticalTabStripBottomContainer below the
NewTabButton, where IsPositionInWindowCaption() returns true
(HTCAPTION).

On Windows, moving the mouse over an HTCAPTION area generates
WM_NCMOUSEMOVE instead of WM_MOUSEMOVE, causing ui_controls'
InputDispatcher::CreateForMouseMove to time out after 10 seconds while
HWNDMessageHandler has already converted WM_NCMOUSEMOVE into a mouse
move event and fired kExpandOnHoverComplete long before
WaitForExpandOnHover() starts listening.

Fix this by:
- Moving the mouse to kOmniboxElementId before collapsing the tab strip
  so the cursor starts outside the vertical tab strip region.
- Moving the mouse to kTabStripElementId (HTCLIENT) in parallel with
  WaitForExpandOnHover().
- Re-subscribing to BrowserAnimationController after enabling vertical
  tabs so VerticalTabStripRegionView updates its state before test
  custom events fire, and ensuring rich animations are enabled.

[chromecast] Validate AudioConfig in ProxyCallTranslator

Validate AudioConfig in ProxyCallTranslator::SetConfig using
IsValidConfig and reject unmapped codecs (kCodecDTSXP2, kCodecDTSE) and
sample formats (kSampleFormatPlanarU8) before translating to gRPC types.
This prevents invalid configurations from triggering NOTREACHED crashes
or downstream out-of-bounds accesses.

ce6f39e93266093cd1652a446b8b5c9f073e2da8 [ diff ] 
Remove wexit_time_destructors config from //chrome/browser

Since is_wexit_time_destructors_default is enabled by default for
Chromium builds, -Wexit-time-destructors is already part of
//build/config/compiler:default_warnings. This makes
//build/config/compiler:wexit_time_destructors a no-op. Remove all
redundant references to wexit_time_destructors in //chrome/browser
BUILD.gn files.

Move CreateSubresourceFilterWebContentsHelper to TabFeatures

Moves `CreateSubresourceFilterWebContentsHelper` out of
`TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers` into `TabFeatures::Init` and
`TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents` on Desktop, and into
`TabFeatures::TabFeatures` on Android. Also initializes it on pre-tab
and non-tab surfaces that create subresource filter navigation
throttles before `TabFeatures` is attached
(`PrerenderWebContentsDelegate`, `ChromeThinWebViewInitializer`,
`ChromeExtensionHostDelegate::CreateTab` on Android, and
`TabWebContentsDelegateAndroid::AddNewContents`).

b1c0abe0d8c248e9c87bfdfa13e836b4755b5301
parent
5b4cbd948bad542a673b2e81c674846aa6e24520 [ diff ] 
[VIZ] Prevent render pass backing padding from bleeding

Render pass backing textures can be larger than their logical content
because allocation sizes are rounded or backings are reused.

SkiaRenderer previously treated the entire physical image as valid
content. When a render pass was upscaled with linear filtering, sampling
could reach transparent allocation padding and expose content underneath
along the right or bottom edge.

Constrain valid texture sampling to the referenced source render pass's
output_rect. Apply the same bounds to regular render pass drawing and
render pass overlay preparation.

Rebaseline affected pixel tests to account for the corrected render-pass
edge sampling.

Gate the behavior behind the RenderPassBackingLogicalBounds feature flag
enabled by default to provide a kill switch.

Move ChromeTranslateClient to TabFeatures

Move ChromeTranslateClient::CreateForWebContents from
TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers to Desktop TabFeatures::Init, Desktop
TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents, and Android TabFeatures::TabFeatures.

ChromeTranslateClient remains a WebContentsUserData (added to
_ALLOWED_CREATE_FOR_CALLS in chrome/browser/ui/tabs/PRESUBMIT.py)
because it is looked up on WebContents across //chrome/browser and
detaches from ContentTranslateDriver in WebContentsDestroyed() after
TabFeatures is destroyed.

Remove wexit_time_destructors config from //remoting

Since is_wexit_time_destructors_default is enabled by default for

//build/config/compiler:default_warnings. This makes
//build/config/compiler:wexit_time_destructors a no-op. Remove all
redundant references to wexit_time_destructors in //remoting BUILD.gn
files.

[omnibox] Fix selection contrast and narrow Realbox for URL mirror impl
📋 Metadata
commit
21c64ea59e2c210b6ab1cec09c14a47cab06dc64
[ log ]
author
Khalid Peer <khalidpeer@chromium.org>
Mon Oct 12 20:11:14 2026
committer

Mon Oct 12 20:11:14 2026
tree
87483ae8b46f285ce86be5d4bf977484d6e1292c
parent
c54bf53343b73361c6b622bed03822f65e50e18d [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Fix selection contrast and narrow Realbox for URL mirror impl

crrev.com/c/8423010 added a #mirror overlay above the searchbox input to
render URL emphasis. This caused two regressions:

1. Low-contrast selection in the WebUI omnibox popup. When text is
   selected (e.g. "chrome://flags" after focusing the omnibox), it
   showed up as dark text on the dark blue selection highlight, which is
   nearly unreadable. This happened because the input's ::selection
   color was forced to transparent and the mirror was stacked above the
   input. The mirror drew its normal (non-selected) text colors on top
   of the input's selection highlight.

Fix: Stack the mirror beneath the input (z-index 99 vs. 100) and stop
   forcing ::selection text to be transparent. Unselected input text
   stays transparent, so the mirror still renders it with URL emphasis.
   Selected text and its highlight are now drawn by the input above the
   mirror, using the omnibox selection colors as before
   crrev.com/c/8423010. The shared searchbox CSS now sets explicit
   ::selection colors for url-emphasis-enabled, so selected text never
   inherits the input's transparent color.

2. Realbox overflow in narrow windows. On the New Tab Page, the Realbox
   uses a <textarea> with field-sizing: content. In narrow windows, the
   textarea no longer shrank, so the "AI Mode" button overflowed past
   the right edge of the searchbox. The textarea is now wrapped in
   #mirrorContainer, a flex item with the default min-width: auto. That
   made the container's minimum width the textarea's min-content width
   (e.g. the placeholder width when force-single-line is set), even
   though the textarea itself has min-width: 0. This applies whether or
   not URL emphasis is enabled.

Fix: Add min-width: 0 to #mirrorContainer. This restores the
   shrinking behavior the input had as a direct flex child before
   crrev.com/c/8423010.

Tests: SearchboxInputTest now checks that (a) with URL emphasis, the
mirror is stacked below the input and ::selection text is not
transparent, (b) the input element doesn't render a background that
could cover the #mirror layer, and (c) a multi-line input with long text
shrinks so that a slotted action button stays inside a narrow searchbox.

Move content_settings::PageSpecificContentSettings to TabFeatures

Move content_settings::PageSpecificContentSettings creation from
TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers to Desktop TabFeatures::Init, Desktop
TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents, and Android TabFeatures::TabFeatures,
as well as non-tab and pre-tab WebContents creation points in
ChromeThinWebViewInitializer::AttachTabHelpers,
PrerenderWebContentsDelegateImpl::PrerenderWebContentsCreated,
ChromeExtensionHostDelegate::CreateTab, and
TabWebContentsDelegateAndroid::AddNewContents.

content_settings::PageSpecificContentSettings remains attached to
WebContents (added to _ALLOWED_CREATE_FOR_CALLS in
chrome/browser/ui/tabs/PRESUBMIT.py) because it lives in
//components/content_settings/browser and is also attached to non-tab
and pre-tab WebContents.

Match bookmark widget preview background and border corner radii

bookmark_widget_preview_layout layered @drawable/hairline_border
(which uses @dimen/widget_corner_radius, resolving to
system_app_widget_background_radius on Android 12+) on top of
@drawable/bookmark_widget_layout_preview, whose vector background path
had a hardcoded 10dp corner radius. On launchers where the widget
picker surface contrasts with @color/widget_bg (such as Samsung One UI),
the 10dp background corners protruded outside the hairline border.

Remove the hardcoded 10dp background path from
bookmark_widget_layout_preview and use @drawable/bookmark_widget_background
(which also uses @dimen/widget_corner_radius) as the preview layout
background.

Before: http://screen/3jW3WYcQRNqRxJq.png
After: http://screen/AEukP5PBwv55CSo.png

tree
030cd2c303ed9f2bf04eaf40df8b472650016c33
parent
bb2c2d5a36f1d786e67033040ab9098c69b1603e [ diff ] 
[ChroMenu] Make tab groups searchable in app menu

Previously, app menu search indexed the items inside each saved tab
group instead of the group itself.

Per the design, a tab group result is a shortcut to open or focus the
group, without the menu's tab group actions. Now each group is one
result, and its tabs and the tab group actions are no longer indexed. To
open the group, its action item now has an invoke callback and stores
the group's saved UUID.

WebUI Toolbar: Fix battery saver button browsertests

With WebUI Toolbar / WebUI Battery Saver Button enabled, the Views
BatterySaverButton is not created in ToolbarView. Tests in
battery_saver_button_browsertest.cc were dereferencing null pointers
when querying the Views button directly.

Modernize BatterySaverHelpPromoTest and BatterySaverBubbleViewTest using
Kombucha test sequences (InteractiveFeaturePromoTest and
InteractiveBrowserTest) to interact with the button and bubble via
tracked element identifiers, supporting both Views and WebUI toolbars.

[Focus] Fix Crash when Ungrouping Focused Ephemeral Tab Group

Ungrouping tabs in a focused ephemeral tab group causes ExitFocusMode to
recursively ungroup and delete the ephemeral group while
RemoveFromGroupImpl is still executing.

To solve this, checks are added within RemoveFromGroupImpl to verify the
group still exists before continuing. Additionally,
MaybeUpdateFocusModeForMovedTab is deferred until after the initial
notification loop in MoveTabsWithNotifications to ensure the group tab
count is updated first.

Test: *.UngroupingFocusedEphemeralGroupDissolvesIt

tree
f1648f6071d44e20f87b99d2a2466c4d6b31d634
parent
0c5fb7141025d364c5bb6e69068b7126616287e8 [ diff ] 
content: sync MediaSession source icons on primary page change

This is a speculative fix for a CSP error on WebUI pages that they load
images from forbidden origins (i.e. HTTPs origins, since WebUIs are not
allowed to load network resources).

The suspect is MediaSessionImpl. This class stores the favicon URL that
is shown by the Global Media Controller (GMC) UI whenever there's media
playing. MediaSessionImpl does not refresh its cached favicon URL on
navigations, and instead relies on the page reporting its favicon via
DidUpdateFaviconURL(). However, WebUI pages never report favicons (they
have no <link rel=icon>). This means, if there's a navigation from
https://www.google.com => chrome://new-tab-page, and then the NTP
attempts to play media (e.g. from an animated doodle?), the GMC will
attempt to retrieve google.com's favicon, and trigger the CSP error.

This CL syncs the source icons with the new primary page's favicons in
PrimaryPageChanged(), like Initialize() does. They are empty for a new
document, which reports its favicons once loaded, and the existing ones
for a page restored from the back/forward cache, which doesn't report
them again.

TAG=agy

Bug: 449515858
Test: content_browsertests --gtest_filter=MediaSessionImplBrowserTest.UpdateFaviconURL_ClearOnNavigateToPageWithoutFavicons

e6d738614572a2ceb6ac56780079e08877d2ff8a
parent
9162a40091c4b1bc99cc542de72d71b39fa56729 [ diff ] 
[WebAPK] Open replacement NTP on last tab and harden tab reparenting

1. Open a replacement NTP when reparenting the last tab in a TabModel:
   On Desktop Android (where Grid Tab Switcher is disabled and closing
   with zero tabs is off), detaching the last tab in a browser window
   leaves the window open in a tabless state with a stale omnibox URL
   and hidden new-tab button. If the reparented tab is the last tab in
   its TabModel, open a replacement NTP via TabCreatorUtil.launchNtp()
   before detaching, matching Desktop Chrome's
   ReparentWebContentsIntoAppBrowser(), so the TabModel never
   transiently drops to zero tabs.

2. Detach the tab only after the WebappActivity launch succeeds:
   Split reparenting into two phases.
   WebApkReparentingHandler#getTabIdForReparenting(Intent) validates
   the one-shot token, the WebAPK package and start URL, that the tab
   is still in scope, and the tab state, without detaching. If the token
   matches but validation fails, it clears the stored state so the token
   can't be reused; a bounce without the matching token is ignored.
   WebappLauncherActivity skips reparenting if a WebappActivity for the
   app is already running, since onNewIntent() doesn't adopt reparented
   tabs. After safeStartActivity() succeeds, it calls
   detachAndRegisterTabAndClear(int), which prunes pre-scope navigation
   history, opens the replacement NTP, and detaches the tab; if the
   launch fails, it calls clear() instead, so the tab stays in its
   window.

3. Only reparent regular browser tabs that can be shown as is:
   WebApkReparentingHandler#canReparentTab() only allows tabs in the
   main browser app (Tab#isTabInBrowser()), skipping Custom Tab, TWA
   and WebappActivity tabs, whose activity manages its own tab and
   can't host a replacement NTP, and off-the-record tabs (WebappActivity
   always uses a regular profile). Since WebappActivity skips the
   initial load for a reparented tab, it also requires the tab to be
   attached and loaded: not closing, detached, frozen, needing reload,
   crashed (SadTab), or showing an error page. It is checked when the
   token is attached and again when it is validated, and mTabObserver
   clears the pending state if the tab is closed, detached, crashed, or
   destroyed. Skipped installs fall back to a normal WebAPK launch.

4. Clear stale EXTRA_SKIP_LOAD_ON_REPARENTING when not reparenting:
   In CustomTabActivityTabController, clear
   EXTRA_SKIP_LOAD_ON_REPARENTING from the intent whenever the initial
   tab is not a reparented tab, i.e. when no reparenting params exist in
   AsyncTabParamsManagerSingleton (e.g. on cold relaunch from Android
   Recents) or when a speculated hidden tab is used, so the initial URL
   still loads.

Bug: 560174165
Test: WebappLauncherActivityTest, WebApkInstallNotificationTest, CustomTabActivityUrlLoadingTest

[Cast] Report capture feedback and stats when the encoder drops a frame

When the video encoder drops a frame (e.g. libvpx rate control in
realtime mode), it emits an empty output. VideoSender previously
returned early in that case without sending VideoCaptureFeedback,
updating the encoder utilization, or recording the drop anywhere.

During a sustained run of encoder drops, VideoCaptureOracle therefore
received no consumer feedback, considered its feedback stale after one
second, and stopped adjusting the capture resolution, which is exactly
when it should step down. Additionally, Open Screen's
NumFramesDroppedByEncoder statistic (shown as "Dropped by Encoder" in
chrome://media-router-internals) was always zero, because nothing called
openscreen::cast::Sender::ReportFrameDropEvent().

This change:
- Reports full (1.0, then attenuated) resource utilization for frames
  dropped by the encoder, so the oracle can reduce the capture size.
- Updates the measured encoder utilization, but not lossiness, which
  is meaningless for an empty output.
- Adds FrameSender::ReportFrameDroppedByEncoder(), which
  OpenscreenFrameSender forwards to Sender::ReportFrameDropEvent(), so
  NumFramesDroppedByEncoder is populated.
- Counts encoder drops in a new VideoSender::GetFramesDroppedByEncoder()
  rather than GetFramesDropped(). The latter tracks sender-side drops
  and gates bandwidth reductions in OpenscreenSessionHost; counting
  rate-control drops there could create a feedback loop of ever lower
  bitrates.
- Records a new CastStreamingFrameDropReason::kDroppedByEncoder UMA
  bucket, and adds the missing kInvalidReferencedFrameId bucket to
  enums.xml.
- Factors the attenuation and key frame capping into
  ReportResourceUtilization().

Bug: 571299211

tree
a8af562ae58fc5b404687bd85d7e94d70095453e
parent
69cb8873752e52b5c4cbe8325d3faf507a87d158 [ diff ] 
blink: Route EME timers and promises to TaskType::kMediaElementEvent

During the initial per-frame scheduler migration (crbug.com/624694), the
Blink EME timers in MediaKeys, MediaKeySession, and SetMediaKeysHandler
were placed on TaskType::kMiscPlatformAPI (a deferrable queue) because
kInternalMedia did not exist yet and kMediaElementEvent had not yet been
exempted from deferral.

When crrev.com/c/574713 later moved TaskType::kMediaElementEvent onto a
non-deferrable queue for smooth playback,
MediaKeySession::async_event_queue_ was upgraded automatically, while
MediaKeySession::action_timer_, MediaKeys::timer_, and
SetMediaKeysHandler::timer_ stayed behind on kMiscPlatformAPI. Later,
when crrev.com/c/1400430 audited V8 execution on the thread-default task
runner for bfcache (crbug.com/870606), only
MediaKeySystemAccess::createMediaKeys() moved to
TaskType::kInternalMedia.

Align all remaining timers and promise resolutions in
third_party/blink/renderer/modules/encryptedmedia/
(MediaKeySystemAccess, MediaKeys, MediaKeySession, and
SetMediaKeysHandler) onto TaskType::kMediaElementEvent alongside
MediaKeySession::async_event_queue_.

Bug: 571966721

6abaefb21fa362f64e1224057d962957eda23612
parent
807b25dcad9a1ebcd1de56ef535a8f83c3a4163a [ diff ] 
Read staged Android theme in CrossDeviceSettingImporter

CrossDeviceThemeTracker on Android only tracks cross-OS themes (Desktop
and iOS), not THEMES_ANDROID. When the best-match device is Android,
getThemeFromRemoteDevice previously returned null, preventing Undo from
restoring the prior local theme and disabling THEMES sync.

When bestMatch.osType is OsType.ANDROID and theme sync is active, read
the staged incoming Android theme directly from
NtpCustomizationConfigManager#getSyncedNtpBackgroundData instead of
querying CrossDeviceThemeTracker.

Also promote staged Android themes via
maybeApplyBackgroundUpdateFromDeviceSync (avoiding Sync echoes), gate
applyThemeSettings in applySettings on importedSettingHasThemeChange,
and gate hadThemeChange on isThemeImportSnackbarEnabled in
observation-only mode.

Bug: 517615321
Test: CrossDeviceSettingImporterUnitTest

Reselect previously selected line after deleting match in full omnibox

Deleting a match, e.g. by selecting it with arrow keys and then pressing
Shift+Delete, would previously reset the popup selection (virtual focus)
to the top/default match. With this change, the selection is restored
to the line at which deletion took place, preserving continuity and
avoiding some match confusion.

Fixed: b:562466289

Reland "Do not flush protocol commands while interrupting JS execution"

This reverts commit 2dd9891b7869be9b32266185425e9c2394985b45.

Reason for revert: re-landing with a workaround added in test: the test now calibrates the size of input used to cause a slow regexp match to the machine it's running on. As it happens, JS regeps are slower by a factor of few magnitudes with is_msan=true.

As a drive-by, incorporate crrev.com/c/8545551 in reland.

MOVE_TO_LAST_TAB out of KeyboardShortcuts.onKeyDown() and into
ChromeTabbedActivity.onMenuOrKeyboardAction().

preparation for migrating to the unified C++ accelerator table.

[Keyboard Shortcuts] Route toolbar shortcuts via onMenuOrKeyboardAction

Move inline shortcut handling for FOCUS_APP_MENU_BUTTON,
KEYBOARD_FOCUS_TOOLBAR, FOCUSED_TAB_STRIP_ITEM_REORDER_LEFT,
FOCUSED_TAB_STRIP_ITEM_REORDER_RIGHT, and TOGGLE_MULTISELECT out of

table, where matched accelerators will dispatch command IDs through
MenuOrKeyboardActionController.onMenuOrKeyboardAction().

[AIM] Read the Omnibox hint page from LocationBarDataProvider

The contextual tasks hint describes the page currently shown in the tab,
but HintTextUpdater read it from the per tab session input and relied on
FuseboxSessionState.from() re-stamping that input from the current tab
on every call. Read the page URL and title from the
LocationBarDataProvider instead, and look up the FuseboxSessionState
only where it is needed, when resolving the tool hint for an active AIM
request.

This removes the last hint text reader that depended on the re-stamp
side effect, which prepares for making FuseboxSessionState.from() a pure
accessor.

161caee7a62b099ad9664bc68dace72dba09703d [ diff ] 
Fix Android Web Speech API continuous mode, partials, and confidence

Fix several Web Speech API regressions on Android:

1. In continuous mode on Android T+, request a segmented session via
   EXTRA_SEGMENTED_SESSION paired with a 5-minute
   EXTRA_SPEECH_INPUT_MINIMUM_LENGTH_MILLIS so the recognizer delivers
   finalized utterances through onSegmentResults() (or onPartialResults
   flagged with "final_result" before Android T) and ends the session
   via onEndOfSegmentedSession() without prematurely terminating after
   a single utterance.
2. In onPartialResults(), only mark results as provisional when
   "final_result" is not true, and append EXTRA_UNSTABLE_TEXT to the
   stable hypothesis so in-progress words are included in interim
   transcripts. Ignore provisional partial results when interimResults
   is false.
3. When the recognition service omits confidence scores or reports all
   zeros (as the on-device recognizer does), report 1.0f confidence for
   each hypothesis to match desktop on-device speech recognition and
   prevent web apps from treating valid results as zero-confidence.
4. Guard speech state transitions so duplicate start/end events are not
   emitted across segments or after audio capture ends.

Fixed: b:514521221, b:557399139, b:539852231
Test: content_junit_tests --test-filter=*SpeechRecognitionImplTest*

Pin Android SDK Platform-Tools back to 37.0.0

Platform-Tools 37.0.1 (from crrev.com/c/8547760) breaks physical-device
Android bots: after a device reboot, `adb wait-for-device` times out,
the device is denylisted and the run ends in NoDevicesError.

The failures start at r1715643 on every affected builder; their
regression ranges intersect at (r1715634, r1715643]. The same Pixel 7
still passes the same reboot flow on chromium-m156, which pins
Platform-Tools 37.0.0.

Affected builders:
- Setup reboot: android-14-arm64-rel, android-10-arm64-rel,
  android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel, android-arm64-proguard-rel
- Retries (chrome project): android-14-arm64-rel-tests,
  android-arm-tests, android-arm64-tests

Platform API 37.2 is kept, since crrev.com/c/8559099 already uses
NetworkSecurityPolicy.DOMAIN_ENCRYPTION_MODE_REQUIRED.

Failed build:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-arm64-rel/20126/overview

parent
98844b1d50d142cdb82f2cf3f72ebbed849051c1 [ diff ] 
[context-input] Improve Lens.IdentityDelegation UMA for AccountError

Lens.IdentityDelegation.FetchHeadersStatus does not say why a fetch
resolved to Account Error, which makes the
LensComposeboxIdentityDelegation ramp hard to read.

Production callers always pass an authuser index (0 by default), so
upload auth is driven by the Gaia cookie jar rather than the Chrome
profile. This adds:
- Lens.IdentityDelegation.AccountErrorReason: why the cookie jar
  account at the authuser index was unusable (index out of range,
  cookie account signed out, invalid session, or persistent auth
  error). Persistent auth error is the only reason that depends on
  Chrome's refresh token state rather than the cookie jar.
- Lens.IdentityDelegation.AccountErrorReason.{CookieJarFreshness}:
  the same reason split by whether the cached ListAccounts data was
  fresh, to spot errors caused by stale cookie jar data.
- Lens.IdentityDelegation.FetchHeadersStatus.{ChromeSignInState}:
  FetchHeadersStatus split by whether the profile is signed into
  Chrome. Sign-in state does not affect account selection; the split
  verifies that Chrome signed-out users with valid web cookies succeed
  at a similar rate.
- Lens.IdentityDelegation.CookieJarAccountCount and
  Lens.IdentityDelegation.CookieJarAccountsFresh: cookie jar size and
  whether the cached ListAccounts data was fresh.

Account selection behavior is unchanged.

7e6b67d1d69086e5fdfc033e1c67bd8629dbef56 [ diff ] 
[chromecast] Fix heap OOB write in AudioSinkAndroidAudioTrackImpl

This fixes a potential heap out-of-bounds write in ReformatData by
comparing the data size as size_t instead of casting it to a signed int.
The signed cast could allow very large values (>= 2GB) to pass the
CHECK_LT check because they become negative, leading to a buffer
overflow.

parent
bd9cf54a592ea8324a06685dc6be48f217243d5c [ diff ] 
[Reading mode] Replace simple-action-menu's index with selectedValue

simple-action-menu now takes the data of the selected item as a
selectedValue prop and compares each item's data against it to show the
checkmark, instead of each menu converting its prop to an index first.
Clicking an item only fires the event; the checkmark moves when the
parent passes the new value back down.

The color, line spacing, letter spacing, font, rate, highlight and
presentation menus bind their props directly, which removes the
getIndexOfSetting/getIndexOrDefault helpers. Tests check the rendered
checkmark via a getCheckedIndex helper rather than the removed index.

b7319baa74e502abd5a6bd022691472faa2a2666

Mon Oct 12 19:04:41 2026
tree
71e9bb192a9de7d8e728ebeb2419cd9049999a67
parent
d5da7c5f5dc6b3a9a7bf1a75ee07d87aa3412872 [ diff ] 
Return std::string from base::debug::ReadElfBuildId()

Simplify ReadElfBuildId() to return std::string directly instead of
writing to a fixed-size out-parameter (ElfBuildIdBuffer) and returning
the byte length, and remove ElfBuildIdBuffer.

Historically, ReadElfBuildId() returned base::Optional<std::string>
until https://crrev.com/c/1474251 (2019), which switched to a
fixed-size buffer with comments stating that elf_reader.cc may be used
in crash-handling / signal-handler code and must avoid dynamic memory
allocation.

However, none of the functions in elf_reader.cc (ReadElfBuildId(),
ReadElfLibraryName(), GetElfProgramHeaders(), GetRelocationOffset())
are used in crash handling or signal handlers:
- stack_trace_posix.cc (which implements the in-process crash signal
  handler) does not use elf_reader.cc at all.
- stack_trace_fuchsia.cc delegates crash stack dumping to the system
  crashlogger (Fuchsia does not use POSIX signal handlers), and
  StackTrace::OutputToStreamWithPrefixImpl() writes to std::ostream
  and LOG(WARNING).
- stack_trace_android.cc only uses ReadElfBuildId() inside
  StackTrace::OutputToStreamWithPrefixImpl(), which already allocates
  std::string and std::vector on the heap.
- All other callers (module_cache_posix.cc, anr_build_id_provider.cc,
  os_metrics_linux.cc, tracing_sampler_profiler.cc) immediately
  convert the buffer into a heap-allocated std::string.

Returning std::string directly via HexEncode() / HexEncodeLower()
simplifies all callers and removes manual buffer sizing.

3958469173e2125ff6f9bbb9f2bc2784d834f5a1
parent
44b0fb43e844670603be16f775f12f36dc44fbe7 [ diff ] 
Fix clipboard history crash when clicking unselected item

When a user clicks on the main button of a clipboard history item,
MenuController forwards mouse press and release events directly to
ClipboardHistoryMainButton without updating menu item selection. If the
clicked item was not already selected by mouse hover (for example, when
the cursor is already over the item or selection moved away via keyboard
navigation), its pseudo_focus_ remains kEmpty.

Previously, HandleMainButtonPressEvent only updated pseudo_focus_ to
kMainButton for kGestureTap events, leaving pseudo_focus_ as kEmpty on
mouse release and hitting DUMP_WILL_BE_NOTREACHED() in
CalculateActionForMainButtonClick(). Update HandleMainButtonPressEvent
to also set pseudo_focus_ to kMainButton for mouse events.

[Omnibox] Fix and re-enable @DisabledTest methods in NavigateTest

Re-enable the remaining @DisabledTest methods in NavigateTest:
- testNavigateMany:
  1. Wait for mOmnibox.checkFocus(false) after observer.assertLoaded()
     in typeInOmniboxAndNavigate so consecutive navigations do not race
     against LocationBarMediator.loadUrl's delayed
     endInputAndFocusCurrentTab task.
  2. Commit text through InputConnection in OmniboxTestUtils#setText
     instead of calling UrlBar#setText and UrlBar#setAutocompleteText
     directly, avoiding TextView#setText restarting input mid-batch-edit
     and setAutocompleteText swallowing the text change notification.
- testOpenAndNavigate: Query tab count on the UI thread via
  mActivityTestRule.tabsCount(false).
- testRendererInitiatedIntentNavigate: Expect an opaque initiator
  origin (per https://crrev.com/c/3966130).

[ios] Do not dismiss Find in Page navigator when inactive

with False events because it is incorrectly logging on every tab switch.

[Keyboard Shortcuts] Enable accelerator_table on Android

In preparation for unifying Android keyboard shortcuts with Desktop's
accelerator table, allow //chrome/browser/ui:accelerator_table and its
unit test (accelerator_table_unittest.cc) to build on Android:
- Remove static_assert(!BUILDFLAG(IS_ANDROID)) from
accelerator_table.cc.
- Remove unused #includes from accelerator_table.cc (including
chrome/browser/ui/tabs/features.h, which comes from a non-Android
target) and prune the corresponding unused deps in
chrome/browser/ui/BUILD.gn.
- Move source_set("accelerator_table") and accelerator_table_unittest.cc
out of `if (!is_android)` blocks in chrome/browser/ui/BUILD.gn.
This CL has no behavioral impact as nothing in production on Android
depends on //chrome/browser/ui:accelerator_table yet.

6588e8330aaa1c793b0ac5138ed2dbc2131140aa
parent
8292d62c984b3e111bf123371c208a1a5872e447 [ diff ] 
[Actor]: Register FileUploadToolRequest in ToolRegistry

actor::ToolRegistry finds tool schemas by calling GetToolDefinition()
on each ToolRequest type in ToolRequestVariant. Each registered tool
declares its model-facing name, description and parameters with
ToolDefinitionBuilder.

FileUploadToolRequest has no tool definition yet, so the registry does
not expose the file upload tool to the model.

This CL adds ToolId::kFileUpload, the file_upload model-facing name,
parameter key constants, and a GetToolDefinition() with a dom_node_id
integer and a file_urls string array. As in ClickToUploadAction, the
target may be a file input or a control that opens the file chooser.
The parameter is named file_urls rather than file_paths because
FileUploadToolRequest only accepts http and https URLs, which the
browser downloads; it rejects local files. In practice these are the
server-issued download URLs of files the user already uploaded.

The schema does not expose FileUploadSource::file_name. Nothing reads
it today; ClickToUploadAction only carries it for display in client
UI. Exposing it would need an array of objects, which ToolSchemaBuilder
does not support.

The tests check the schema and that the registry discovers the tool.

PageContextFetcher: Handle failed PDF bytes extraction from PDFium

PDFium's extraction API can return:
`pdf::mojom::PdfListener::GetPdfBytesStatus::kFailed`. Currently
PageContextFetcher does not handle that. The PDF result will contain
an empty vector.

- When the failed status is returned, ensure PDF result is an nullopt.
- Added more comments clarifying the different behavior of text v.s.
  bytes extraction when size limit is exceeded.
- crrev.com/c/8464871 will add a status metric for PDF extractions.

[Actor] Grant search origin in PerformSearch

ExecutionEngine::Act() collects AssociatedOriginGrant() from each
ToolRequest in the action sequence before invoking CreateTool(). Because
PerformSearchToolRequest did not override AssociatedOriginGrant(), it
returned std::nullopt during Act(), and its temporary
NavigateToolRequest created inside CreateTool() was never queried for an
origin grant. When kGlicConfirmNavigationToNewOrigins is enabled and the
active tab is on a different origin than the default search provider,
OriginGatingChecker blocked or prompted on the search navigation.

Override PerformSearchToolRequest::AssociatedOriginGrant() to resolve
the profile's default search provider URL from the target tab's Profile
and return its origin when valid and non-opaque. Extract
ResolveSearchUrl() so CreateTool() and AssociatedOriginGrant() share
search URL resolution logic, and add cross-origin navigation gating
browser tests.

R=nandrosz@chromium.org, woodchip@chromium.org

[X11] Restore timeout for in-flight selection requests
📋 Metadata

431b8be6a11100566c2ade47e6f048e955d4bbcd

tree
c2ef69313af23b818d1178ce094a0285cb598829
parent
497d3fd40d69f9e1c1b4bfaf1212c38e44e51579 [ diff ] 
[X11] Restore timeout for in-flight selection requests

SelectionRequester processes ConvertSelection requests serially, so a
selection owner that never replies (e.g. one that exits after the
SelectionRequest is forwarded but before sending SelectionNotify) or an
INCR transfer that stalls would block every subsequent request for the
lifetime of the process. Since r1599052 removed the request timeout,
pasting content from other applications could hang permanently until
the browser was restarted.

This restores a timeout, with a few changes from the original:
- The timeout only applies to the request that has been sent to the
  selection owner, and starts when it is sent rather than when it is
  queued.
- It is reset whenever an INCR transfer makes progress.
- It is 5 seconds rather than 1 second, since the clipboard is now
  asynchronous and the timeout only needs to guarantee forward progress.

Since only the front request can now complete, CompleteRequest() is
simplified and the unused per-request completed/success state is
removed.

Speed up SoftwareVideoEncoderTest by reducing round-trip test frames

SoftwareVideoEncoderTest.EncodeAndDecode and
SoftwareVideoEncoderTest.EncodeAndDecodeWithEnablingDrop encode,
decode, and pixel-compare 250 frames (10s of video at 25fps) across 43
codec, profile, and pixel format parameter sets. Together they take
~85s out of ~125s on `Linux Tests` (~68% of fixture runtime). Over 95%
of the time is spent in encoder and decoder execution.

This CL reduces both tests to 4s of video (100 frames) using a shared
`kEncodeDecodeClipDurationSeconds` constant.

Because the keyframe interval is 3s (75 frames at 25fps), 100 frames
still fully verify the initial keyframe, subsequent delta frames, a
periodic keyframe at frame 75, and delta frames across the keyframe
boundary. In `EncodeAndDecodeWithEnablingDrop`, 0 frames are dropped
under either 250 or 100 frames across all 43 parameter sets due to the
generous 1Mbps bitrate for 320x200 video.

Locally, the 86 `EncodeAndDecode*` tests sped up from 67.0s to 13.9s
(4.8X speedup), reducing total fixture wall time from 112.3s to 33.2s
(saving ~79s).

Enable partial translation for untranslatable PDFs

Previously, untranslatable PDFs were rejected in CanManuallyTranslate(),
which prevented partial translation from being available on those
documents.

This change moves the untranslatable PDF check to TranslatePage(). If
full-page translation is requested for an untranslatable PDF, an error
UI is displayed while allowing CanManuallyTranslate() to remain enabled
for partial translation.

[ios] Add architectural guide for Coordinators

This is a revision and update to the internal (docs) guide for
coordinators. The intent is that this document, in the public repo,
becomes the canonical document; locating it here will make it easier for
humans to refer to in code review, and easier for agents to refer to
when generating code to be reviewed.

This CL doesn't incorporate some recent additions to the coordinator patterns. A follow-up, including discussion of AnimatedCoordinator, ModalHost, and coordinator types will follow after this has landed.

Summary of changes from the internal doc:
* Clarified that coordinators map to logical features rather than 1:1 with `UIViewController`s.
* Added rules on custom initializers, keeping setup out of `-init`, and keeping internal state and protocol conformances in `.mm` files.
* Added a rule against starting dormant, UI-less child coordinators up front.
* Added explicit requirements for deterministic teardown in `-stop`.
* Added guidance for user-driven dismissals in sheet presentations and navigation stacks.
* Expanded coordinator delegate guidance and contrasted it with local commands.
* Added patterns for modal presentation, `UINavigationController` flows, and standard UIKit view controller containment.
* Removed obsolete sections on `ContainedPresenter`, "Legacy/Boxed" coordinators, and speculative `ChromeCoordinatorDelegate` base class helpers.

Additionally:
* Diagrams in the original are converted to svg and included.
* Obsolete comments in ChromeCoordinator are updated.
* Links to the Coordinators doc are added to the Commands doc.

(The Commands doc also got re-line-wrapped).

Fix Media.AudioRendererAudioGlitches2 base histogram name in XML

When a low-latency output stream is destroyed, `OutputGlitchCounter`
records both `Media.AudioRendererAudioGlitches2` and the per-latency
`Media.AudioRendererAudioGlitches2.<LatencyTag>` histogram. Because
`histograms.xml` placed the dot before `{LatencyTag}`, the empty
variant expanded to `Media.AudioRendererAudioGlitches2.` (with a
trailing dot), leaving the base histogram undefined on the UMA
dashboard.

This CL moves the leading dot into the non-empty `<variant>` entries so
the empty variant expands to `Media.AudioRendererAudioGlitches2` while
keeping the 5 per-latency histogram names unchanged.

TAG=agy
CONV=e018e99c-62a0-4241-a020-885e4236ce22

[WebUILocationBar] Fire kControlsChanged for popup state changes and fix OmniboxPopupOpenClose

When WebUILocationBar is enabled,
OmniboxViewViewsDumpAccessibilityEventsTest.OmniboxPopupOpenClose failed
on Windows UIA because WebUILocationBar::OnPopupStateChanged() did not
fire ax::mojom::Event::kControlsChanged (unlike
LocationBarView::OnPopupStateChanged()), and <cr-searchbox-input>
defaulted to role='combobox' instead of role='textbox'.

1. Set textInput.role = 'textbox' in
   ReadonlyOmniboxElement.firstUpdated() to match OmniboxViewViews,
   which sets ax::mojom::Role::kTextField [1].

2. Add WebUILocationBar::NotifyPopupControlsChanged() and call it from
   OnPopupStateChanged() to fire kControlsChanged on the WebUI omnibox
   AXPlatformNode.

3. Use GetTargetRootView()->GetWidget() in
   DumpAccessibilityEventsViewsTestBase::WaitForPendingSerialization().
   Subclasses that test the browser window (such as
   OmniboxViewViewsDumpAccessibilityEventsTest) override
   GetTargetRootView() to return the BrowserView's root view [2] rather
   than using DumpAccessibilityEventsViewsTestBase::widget_ [3].
   StopRecordingAndCompare() fires kEndOfTest on GetTargetRootView()
   [4], which queues a pending serialization on
   GetTargetRootView()->GetWidget()->ax_manager() [5], so
   WaitForPendingSerialization() must wait on that widget's AX manager
   rather than widget_->ax_manager() [6].

   This is technically fixing a pre-existing problem that's not specific
   to WebUI, but the change in timing due to WebUI's asynchronous AX
   serializations exposes that the test was waiting on the wrong widget.

4. Wait for omnibox focus and (when WebUILocationBar is enabled) WebUI
   toolbar accessibility tree initialization before recording events in
   OmniboxPopupOpenClose.

[1]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_view_views.cc;l=250;drc=94dc49ce5c6c5c4228a49cb692000f512a4461d9
[2]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_view_views_interactive_uitest.cc;l=2000-2004;drc=94dc49ce5c6c5c4228a49cb692000f512a4461d9
[3]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/ui/views/accessibility/dump_accessibility_events_views_browsertest_base.cc;l=278-280;drc=94dc49ce5c6c5c4228a49cb692000f512a4461d9
[4]

Fix multi-quantum playout delay reporting in `AudioDestination`

When a device callback holds multiple 128-frame render quanta,
`AudioDestination::RequestRender()` renders them in a loop, but passes
the same `delay_to_report_` to every quantum.

For example, take a 512-frame callback at 48kHz (4 quanta of ~2.67ms
each) with a 20ms initial delay:
- Quantum 0 plays out in 20ms, quantum 1 in ~22.7ms, quantum 2 in
  ~25.3ms, and quantum 3 in ~28ms.
- Today, all 4 quanta get a 20ms delay.

This causes two issues:
1. `AudioContext.playbackStats` under-reports playout latency. In the
   example above, average latency is 20ms instead of 24ms (~4ms too
   low), and max latency is 20ms instead of 28ms (~8ms too low).
2. `AudioDestinationUmaReporter` adds one delay per quantum, but divides
   by `kMetricsReportCycle` (the number of callbacks). In the example
   above, `TotalPlayoutDelay` records 80ms (`4 * 20ms`) per callback
   instead of ~24ms (~4X too high).

This CL offsets `delay_to_report_` by `pushed_frames` on each loop
iteration, and averages the UMA delay over the number of recorded
quanta. Since the UMA values drop significantly, this CL also renames
the histograms to `TotalPlayoutDelay2` to avoid mixing old and new data.

This is a web-exposed behavioral change: `playbackStats` latency goes
up when callbacks hold multiple quanta.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Patterned histogram WebAudio.AudioDestination.{ModeTag}.TotalPlayoutDelay{LatencyTag} is replaced by WebAudio.AudioDestination.{ModeTag}.TotalPlayoutDelay2{LatencyTag}, which no longer inflates the delay by the number of render quanta per callback.

TAG=agy
CONV=189de0c9-75d4-4e82-a5a7-644a2806669c

[iOS][Actor] Record how actor background tasks start and end

Adds journal events and UMA for the actor's background continued
processing tasks:

1. IOS.Actor.BackgroundTask.EndReason: why the background task ended.
The actor task stopped with success or failure, entered a long wait
(waiting on the user or paused), was destroyed while holding the task,
or the background task expired or was cancelled from the Live Activity
before the actor task ended.

2. IOS.Actor.BackgroundTask.Duration: how long the actor held the task,
from receiving its context until it ended.

3. IOS.Actor.BackgroundTask.RequestEligibility + IneligibilityReason:
whether an actor task was eligible to request a background task, and
ineligibility reasons if not.

295b82ee47a46fc941c0267f491a190983286174
parent
cb335582b67b5e535988b1a87cf50d976143fb44 [ diff ] 
🌺 [Android] Stop in-flight autocomplete in OmniboxTestUtils#setSuggestions

OmniboxTest#testTabSelectsFirstSuggestionUpdatesUrlBarText has been
failing on android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests at
omnibox.checkSuggestionsShown() because OmniboxTestUtils#setSuggestions
injects synthetic suggestions via listener.onSuggestionsReceived(...)
without stopping in-flight native autocomplete requests triggered by
omnibox.requestFocus(). When the asynchronous native zero-suggest
response arrives immediately after setSuggestions(), it overwrites the
test-injected suggestion list with an empty list.

Call mAutocomplete.stopAutocompleteForTest(AutocompleteStopReason.INTERACTION)
before invoking listener.onSuggestionsReceived(...) in
OmniboxTestUtils#setSuggestions so in-flight native autocomplete
requests cannot clobber synthetic test suggestions.

Example failing build:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests/1833

[TabUI] Move supportsTabGroups to TabListConfig

Whether a tab list surface supports displaying tab groups is a static
surface capability rather than dynamic layout delegate state. Move
supportsTabGroups from TabListLayoutDelegate into TabListConfig so
TabListMediator can query it directly from mTabListConfig without
routing through TabListLayoutDelegate. This is a no-op refactoring with
no behavior change.

a7000113ad2995db0ed7b306c21f8261af1c6e17

c3555de062f483a1783528b328de8094cafef4d7 [ diff ] 
Fix CommandLine data race in DeepScanningReportingAutomationTest

`DeepScanningReportingAutomationTest` mutates the process command line
in `SetUp()` after background threads have started, and its
`ScopedCommandLine` is destroyed before `BrowserTaskEnvironment` stops
them. Under TSan, this races with background reads and causes test
timeouts.

This CL moves `ScopedCommandLine` to the base fixture so it outlives
the task environment, and sets the switch in the constructor before
threads start.

Over the last 7 days, this caused ~2,954 Linux TSan failures and 975 CQ
exonerations across 145 dry runs.

[TabUI] Move updateColorForChildTabs into TabListMediator

Moves NestedLayoutDelegate#updateColorForChildTabsInNestedLayout into
TabListMediator#updateColorForChildTabs alongside
updateTabGroupColorViewProvider. Under TabUI data virtualization,
TabListLayoutDelegate is bypassed for group color updates
(PayloadType.COLOR), so TabListMediator needs direct access to sync
group color updates down to expanded child tab models in mModelList.

Add DeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker to PasswordManagerClient

This CL updates PasswordManagerClient and its implementations to declare
and implement GetDeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker() and
IsReauthBeforeFillingRequired(DeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker*). The
legacy and new code should be the same with the exception of the object
they call. The legacy code calls the authenticator while the new code
should call the checker.

This CL introduces MockDeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker and updates
ChromePasswordManagerClient and IOSChromePasswordManagerClient,
including unit tests for the new client methods.

Callers are not yet migrated in this CL.

8cb91973a6a1a29f6c0385a3654553538778876c [ diff ] 
[AW] Implement Java SharedArrayBuffer postMessage to main frame

- Extend `MessagePayload` (`MessagePayloadType.SHARED_ARRAY_BUFFER`) and
  `blink::WebMessagePayload` (on Android) to carry a
  `base::UnsafeSharedMemoryRegion` converted from Java `SharedMemory` via
  `ASharedMemory_dupFromJava`.
- Add an Android-only 2-argument `blink::EncodeWebMessagePayload` overload
  that serializes a `kSharedArrayBufferTag` (index 0) and populates the
  `UnsafeSharedMemoryRegion` side-table, while keeping the 1-argument
  overload for renderer-to-renderer `TransferableMessage` callers.
- Expose `content::android::ReleaseJavaMessagePort` so embedders can
  extract `blink::MessagePortDescriptor`s when constructing custom frame
  messages.
- Route `SHARED_ARRAY_BUFFER` payloads in
  `AwContents#postMessageToMainFrame` through
  `AwContents::PostSharedArrayBufferMessageToMainFrame` ->
  `AwRenderViewHostExt::PostEmbedderMessageEvent` after verifying that
  the target `RenderFrameHost` is cross-origin isolated.

[Signin][Android] Introduce IdentityManager refresh tokens loading state

Replaces IdentityManager#areRefreshTokensLoaded() with
IdentityManager#getRefreshTokensLoadingState() returning an
RefreshTokensLoadingState enum (IN_PROGRESS, SUCCEEDED), and updates
IdentityManager.Observer#onRefreshTokensLoaded() to accept
RefreshTokensLoadingState as a parameter. This is a pure refactoring in
preparation for introducing a FAILED state when device account fetching
fails. This CL is temporary implementation and introduced state enum
should be moved to native part in the future (crbug.com/572579970).

e2b6e6bc4fef51a33ddc4f251e09cbf37d6c8dd3
parent
339064199cec5e45915248a7d6c212fbdceda141 [ diff ] 
Fix race in mixed-content-issue-creation-js-within-oopif.js

The test auto-attached to the cross-origin OOPIF with
waitForDebuggerOnStart: false, so the frame could start loading its
mixed-content script before Network.enable and Audits.enable had
completed on the child session. Depending on timing, the first
MixedContentIssue was reported without its request details (text
mismatch) or the requestWillBeSent / issueAdded events were missed
entirely (timeout).

Pause new targets on attach and only resume them with
Runtime.runIfWaitingForDebugger once both domains are enabled. Passes
20/20 locally; remove the flaky expectation.

[browser_actuator] Support exporting session history as JSON

Expose SessionStreamRecorderFactory::ExportSessionsAsJson() to
chrome://browser-actuator-internals so developers can download or
copy the full, uncapped session history as JSON.

Changes:
- Add ExportSessionsAsValue() and ExportSessionsAsJson() accepting
  session_ids to SessionStreamRecorderFactory.
- Add ExportSessionHistory(array<string> session_ids) to
  BrowserActuatorInternalsUI in browser_actuator_internals.mojom and
  implement it in BrowserActuatorInternalsUIMojoImpl.
- Add "Export JSON" and "Copy JSON" buttons to the header of
  chrome://browser-actuator-internals (disabled when no sessions
  have been recorded), showing a <cr-toast> when copied.
- Add C++ unit tests in
  browser_actuator_internals_ui_mojo_impl_unittest.cc and
  session_stream_recorder_unittest.cc, and WebUI Mocha tests in
  browser_actuator_internals_app_test.ts.

Move InitializePageLoadMetricsForWebContents to TabFeatures

Move InitializePageLoadMetricsForWebContents out of
TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers into Desktop TabFeatures::Init,
TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents, and Android TabFeatures::TabFeatures.

Also initialize page load metrics in the pre-tab and non-tab navigation
entry points (ChromeNoStatePrefetchContentsDelegate,
PrerenderWebContentsDelegateImpl::PrerenderWebContentsCreated,
ChromeExtensionHostDelegate::CreateTab on Android, and
TabWebContentsDelegateAndroid::AddNewContents) so
MetricsWebContentsObserver continues to observe navigations that start
before TabFeatures is initialized.

Remove redundant browser_public_dependencies (batch 6, 5/5)

Now that //chrome/browser:browser has no sources or circular includes
and //chrome/browser/ui:ui has been removed, targets no longer need to
depend on //chrome/browser:browser_public_dependencies to break cycles.
Remove the redundant dependency from 23 BUILD.gn files and add
any direct public dependencies that were previously satisfied
transitively.

443bad90474dd77ae69b9f2bcea4b442c0ef596d [ diff ] 
[android] Ensure line-buffered stdout and flush in ScopedMainEntryLogger

When test stdout is redirected to a file via dup2() in
native_test_launcher.cc, standard libc stdio switches stdout from
line-buffered (_IOLBF) to 4096-byte block-buffered (_IOFBF) because the
underlying file descriptor is not a tty.

When an operation such as --gtest_list_tests or a test suite times out
or crashes, the last 4KB buffer slice flushed to disk can terminate
mid-line (e.g. '  WrapPa' from '  WrapPathWithLTRFormatting\n'). When
Devil retries StartInstrumentation() in append mode ('a+'),
ScopedMainEntryLogger() prints '>>ScopedMainEntryLogger\n' directly
onto the truncated line, corrupting test names into unparsable
identifiers like 'RTLTest.WrapPa>>ScopedMainEntryLogger' and crashing
test_runner.py.

This change:
1. Calls setvbuf(stdout, nullptr, _IOLBF, 0) immediately after
   redirecting stdout in native_test_launcher.cc, guaranteeing that
   stdout remains line-buffered even when writing to a regular file.
2. Calls fflush(stdout) in ScopedMainEntryLogger's constructor to match
   its destructor, ensuring >>ScopedMainEntryLogger is flushed
   immediately to disk.

[Omnibox] Remove flaky OmniboxPedalsRenderTest

Remove OmniboxPedalsRenderTest due to extreme flakiness across Android
CI/CQ builders and low testing value:

- Over the last 7 days, OmniboxPedalsRenderTest recorded 93 failures
  across Android CI builders (45 in testRunChromeSafetyCheckPedal
  LiteMode, 17 in testPlayChromeDinoGamePedal NightMode, 16 in
  testRunChromeSafetyCheckPedal NightMode, and 15 in
  testPlayChromeDinoGamePedal LiteMode; 484 total failures over 14d),
  frequently failing in @After clearFocus() keyboard visibility checks.
- Android render tests are currently not working at all: RenderTestRule
  silently ignores image diffs on all configurations except
  sdk_gphone64_x86_64 SDK 32/36, and on supported configurations Skia
  Gold is failing with 503 Service Unavailable / client timeouts and
  unmaintained golden baselines.
- OmniboxPedalsRenderTest adds no unique coverage: Pedal icon selection
  (including PLAY_CHROME_DINO_GAME custom icon vs default icon), chip
  model binding, view key dispatch, and end-to-end Pedal execution are
  already covered by OmniboxPedalUnitTest, ActionChipsProcessorUnitTest,
  ActionChipsViewUnitTest, and OmniboxPedalsTest.

GlicKeyedService (crrev.com/c/8519141). GetProviderForProfile has no
remaining callers; remove it and its tests.
